- **bbc-analyze**: BBC — design the black boxes of a new project, or of a new part of one, from the requirements, with the user, before any code ({@tasks}/bbc/): the black boxes by responsibility, their contracts, dependencies and flows, the decisions of the architecture. No code is read. The diagram is approved before the details; then /dev:bbc-review, and back here with its findings until the user approves the map. Use when a project, or a part of it, too large for one context window is to be built as black boxes, or the user says bbc analyze, mappa BBC, black box map, scomponi in BB, progetta a black box. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-analyze.md))
- **bbc-audit**: BBC — check whether the code reflects the analysis: each black box's code in its namespace, its contract in the Core as the map writes it, the dependencies only through the Core and only those on the map, the decisions of the map kept. It writes a report, and the findings are fixed by one feature. Use when the roadmap reaches the audit, after BBs were built or changed, or the user says bbc audit, il codice rispecchia la mappa, controlla il codice contro la BBC. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-audit.md))
- **bbc-core**: BBC — plan the Core of the contracts, the first step of the roadmap; on a project with no code yet it is the project's start — the profile, the skeleton, then the Core. Every contract of the map written in the project's Core — the abstract concepts of the whole system, each BB's interfaces and the types they exchange under the BB's namespace — with its fake and its contract tests, so that every BB can then be developed in parallel against it. A TDDAB plan, reviewed, then it STOPS and the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when the roadmap's first step is due, or the user says bbc core, s… ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-core.md))
- **bbc-debug**: BBC — fix a bug that crosses several black boxes. The bug path of /dev:j-bug — LISTEN, DIAGNOSE with Protocol D, PLAN — with ISOLATE done on the map, the BBs' tests run leaves first, then the integration points; then STOPS, the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when a failure spans several BBs and cannot be placed, or the user says bbc debug. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-debug.md))
- **bbc-e2e**: BBC — plan the end-to-end tests of the map's flows: every flow that crosses the black boxes, through the real transport and the real storage, at the level the project has (the API, the user interface). A TDDAB plan, reviewed and ready for /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when the user says bbc e2e, test dei flussi, or /dev:bbc-integrate pointed here. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-e2e.md))
- **bbc-help**: Look up quick reference information for Acorn BBC Micro (Model A, B, B+, Master 128) 6502 development. Read and use the files under `reference/` to answer questions about: ([reyco2000/6502-dissasembler-skill](https://github.com/reyco2000/6502-dissasembler-skill/blob/HEAD/.claude/commands/bbc-help.md) · ★1)
- **bbc-integrate**: BBC — integrate black boxes after one of them changed its contract or they were built apart. Every call between them is checked on both sides of the contract, and a TDDAB plan proves them wired together, real BB with real BB; the map's integration points are updated. Use when the roadmap says built BBs meet, after /dev:bbc-plan changed a contract, or when the user says bbc integrate, integra i BB, test tra i BB. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-integrate.md))
- **bbc-plan**: BBC — plan the development of one black box as a feature. From the BB's definition in the map — its responsibility, its contract, its dependencies — the phases of /dev:j-new-feature scoped to the BB write a TDDAB plan, the cases of the contract as the tests and the implementation behind it, the other BBs as the fakes of the Core, the Core's contract tests run against the real implementation; the plan is reviewed, then it STOPS and the user triggers /dev:j-cvm-exec-plan. Use when the user says bbc plan, sviluppa il BB, pianifica il BB, or names a black box of the roadmap to build. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-plan.md))
- **bbc-refactor**: BBC — change a black box of the map — its responsibility, its contract, a split or a merge, a decision — and then the code that follows it: the phases of a feature up to a reviewed plan, the map changed and approved first. Use when the user says bbc refactor, cambia il BB, sposta la responsabilità, dividi o unisci i BB, or /dev:bbc-audit sent a finding of the map here. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-refactor.md))
- **bbc-review**: BBC — review the analysis: the black-box map against the requirements and the method — the boundaries, the contracts, the connections, the logic of the flows, the requirements and the decisions — with no code read; write what is wrong with where and why, and send it back to /dev:bbc-analyze, until the user approves the map; then propose the roadmap of the development — the Core of the contracts, the BBs (in parallel when the user wants), the integrations, the flows, the audit of the code — approved by the user and written in the map. Use after /dev:bbc-analyze, before working on the BBs, or w… ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-review.md))
- **bbc-work**: BBC — delegate the work on one black box to its own git worktree and agent session. It opens the worktree and its branch, writes there the BB's scope and the Memory Bank, and STOPS with the command that opens a session in that folder; there the BB is developed with /dev:bbc-plan, and closed with /dev:j-close. Several BBs can be delegated at the same time. Use when the user says bbc work, lavora sul BB, delega il BB, or a step of the roadmap sends BBs in parallel. ([0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin](https://github.com/0ics-srls/ai-agent-plugin/blob/HEAD/plugins/dev/commands/bbc-work.md))

- **bec-tdd**: Describe one observable behavior in a focused regression, run it and inspect the expected failure, implement the minimum change, then rerun it and the relevant suite. Bedrock has **two CLI namespaces**: `aws bedrock` for management operations (model access, guardrails, logging configuration) and `aws bedrock-runtime` for inference operations (invoking models). - **bedrock-agentcore**: Amazon Bedrock AgentCore is a managed runtime for deploying AI agents as long-running, stateful sessions. Use these commands to deploy agent runtimes, manage credentials, configure gateway targets, and monitor agent sessions. ([rnavarych/alpha-engineer](https://github.com/rnavarych/alpha-engineer/blob/HEAD/plugins/billy-milligan/commands/adr-review.md) · ★15)
- **billy:adr-status**: Change the status of an Architecture Decision Record. Valid transitions: PROPOSED → ACCEPTED → DEPRECATED → SUPERSEDED. Simple, surgical update — no discussion needed. ([rnavarych/alpha-engineer](https://github.com/rnavarych/alpha-engineer/blob/HEAD/plugins/billy-milligan/commands/adr-status.md) · ★15)
- **billy:adr-supersede**: Mark an existing ADR as superseded and create its replacement. Updates the old ADR's status and creates a new ADR that references the old one. Sequential numbering — never reuses old numbers. ([rnavarych/alpha-engineer](https://github.com/rnavarych/alpha-engineer/blob/HEAD/plugins/billy-milligan/commands/adr-supersede.md) · ★15)
- **billy:argue**: Show all unresolved arguments from team memory. Sasha's favorite command. Lists tracked disagreements with each team member's position. ([rnavarych/alpha-engineer](https://github.com/rnavarych/alpha-engineer/blob/HEAD/plugins/billy-milligan/commands/billy-argue.md) · ★15)
- **billy:context**: Show what the team knows about the user and project. Loads context.md from local Billy memory — accumulated knowledge from past sessions. Update it with /billy:save context "&lt;note&gt;" when new things are learned. ([rnavarych/alpha-engineer](https://github.com/rnavarych/alpha-engineer/blob/HEAD/plugins/billy-milligan/commands/billy-context.md) · ★15)
